def mix(val):
    if val < 2:
        return 2
    else:
        return mix(val // 2) + mix(val - val // 2) + val % 3
print(mix(6))
--- L0#1 -> '3'
def score(res, t):
    if t >= len(res):
        return 1
    elif res[t] % 2 == 0:
        return res[t] + score(res, t + 1)
    else:
        return 2 * score(res, t + 1) - 1
print(score([2, 5, 1, 9], 0))

print(f(3))

============================== C1.union_find_links graphs
--- L0#0 -> '[2, 1, 2, 2, 2]'
def find(p, res):
    while p[res] != res:
        res = p[res]
    return res
p = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]
n = [2, 3, 4]
total = [3, 4, 0]
for t in range(len(n)):
    z = find(p, n[t])
    out = find(p, total[t])
    if z != out:
        p[out] = z
print(p)
--- L0#1 -> '1'
def find(n, count):
    while n[count] != count:
        count = n[count]
    return count
n = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]
c = [4, 4, 4]
k = [0, 3, 2]
for best in range(len(c)):
    e = find(n, c[best])
    p = find(n, k[best])
    if e != p:
        if e < p:
            n[p] = e
        else:
            n[e] = p
total = 0
for best in range(5):
    total += find(n, best)
print(total)

    while x[p] != p:
